## 第 2 轮 — LLM 响应

Key findings so far:
- propId 291504647 = 0x1160_... let me compute: 291504647 = 0x1160_0007? Actually 291504647 in hex = 0x11600007. This is a CarProperty ID.
- The CPECallbackController is **Dropping** carPropertyEvent for propId 291504647 repeatedly — this is a rate-limiting/dedup mechanism.
- Fridge related: `P_VEHICLE_CONTROL_FRIDGE_POWER_DRAWER_ENABLE` and RPC `SetCLEAFridge`.

Let me convert propId and search for the fridge property ID and the SettingsActivity smart tab loading.

- `IntelligentDeviceViewModel` — handles power level etc.
- `StatusBarFridge: support=true, selected=false, status=100, switchOn=false` — fridge status
- `PatacFridgeSignal` — fridge signal

The bug time is 14:19:00, but the log shows IntelligentDeviceFragment activity at 14:21:41. Let me focus on the actual time window around 14:19. Let me search for the fridge signal and IntelligentDeviceFragment around the bug time.

## 第 4 轮 — LLM 响应

Now I have a clear picture. Key findings:

1. **IntelligentDeviceFragment** is created at **14:19:17.740** — this is when the user enters the 智能设备 Tab. This matches the bug time (14:19).
2. **PatacFridgeSignal** shows fridge state: `switch: CFSS_OFF`, `mode: CFMS_Cooling`, `cool_temperature: 10`, `shut_reason: COFSDR_NOT_ENABLED`.
3. The fridge cooling mode is `CFMS_Cooling` but switch is `CFSS_OFF`.

The bug is about the "冰箱制冷选项" (fridge cooling option) flashing highlight. Let me read the raw log around 14:19:17 to understand the fragment creation and the fridge state loading sequence.
